Conclusion: 生命周期撤销应成为智能体记忆的核心操作，使智能体能够证伪、审计并重新提升不断演化的知识。边界测试揭示多跳和超长上下文场景中的瓶颈在于检索链和上下文选择，而非事实级有效性追踪。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables language agents to reuse past facts, preferences, and task experience. Persistence also creates a central falsifiability problem: when the world changes, stale memories can remain retrievable and pollute the prompt. We characterize this failure mode as memory pollution: degradation caused by active memories that newer conflicting evidence has superseded. We introduce TEPA, a revocable evidence-memory mechanism that makes validity an explicit state of memory. TEPA represents observations as keyed precedents and revokes active precedents when fresh evidence contradicts them under the same key, allowing retrieval to draw from current evidence while preserving revoked history for audit. Across controlled hidden-regime drift, real file-backed executable drift, and preference-update streams, revocation prevents stale active memory from remaining in the retrieval set after reversal. In controlled drift over 50 seeds, append-only and last-write-wins memory fell below no memory during full reversal (append-only and last-write-wins both 0.210, no memory 0.309, TEPA 0.950), and the same pattern reproduced under real file execution (append-only 0.203, no memory 0.298, TEPA 0.950). On clean MemoryAgentBench SH-6k, TEPA matches a strong last-write-wins cache, confirming that current-key replacement is the decisive operation for single-hop fact consolidation. Boundary tests on multi-hop and very long-context MemoryAgentBench settings expose retrieval-chain and context-selection bottlenecks beyond fact-level validity tracking. Together, these results establish lifecycle revocation as a core memory operation for agents that must falsify, audit, and later re-promote evolving knowledge.

</details>


### [167] [Post-Grokking Collapse at the Representation-Readout Interface in Muon-Trained Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07436)
*Ali Janati,Kaoutar El Maghraoui,Andrei Kanavalau,Anass Belfatmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon优化器在模加法任务上的"顿悟"（grokking）现象中出现的隐藏矩阵表示与AdamW嵌入/输出头之间的不稳定交互。所有九种配置在$(a+b) \bmod 113$任务上都会在顿悟后失去泛化能力，失败根源在于表示-读出接口处存在未被损失函数选择的可逆映射，导致两种优化器行为不对称（Muon步长弹性为-0.03而AdamW为+1.5），最终通过傅里叶滤波分离出电路失败与掩蔽两种机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解不同优化器（Muon和AdamW）在模加任务上顿悟后的泛化不稳定性，特别是Muon获得的隐藏矩阵与AdamW获得的嵌入/输出头之间的交互如何导致周期性的泛化丢失。

Method: 在$(a+b) \bmod 113$任务上系统比较九种配置，跨五种子、两个模数、两个宽度、两个训练比例、减法和深度进行实验；通过冻结表示或读出、去除Muon的归一化与正交化、傅里叶滤波分析、计算步长弹性和每参数移动速度等手段定位失败机理。

Result: 所有九种配置在顿悟后均出现泛化丢失；AdamW参考在五个种子中四个低于阈值（27.59%）。失败源于表示-读出接口处未被损失选择的可逆映射。冻结任一组可防止失败；去除Muon归一化/正交化导致表示从326个有效共轭对坍缩至4个。任务对齐的傅里叶族单独达到100%，在掩蔽情况下被近相等对抗性余数否决但缩放可恢复99.9%。

Conclusion: 顿悟后的泛化丢失源于表示-读出接口处由损失函数未约束的可逆映射自由度引起，不同优化器对该自由度的不对称响应导致电路失败或掩蔽两种失效模式；冻结表示或读出以及傅里叶族缩放均可稳定修复。

Abstract: Under the standard split, Muon gets hidden matrices and AdamW embeddings/output head. Muon groks modular addition faster, but its solutions do not hold. All nine configurations on $(a+b) \bmod 113$ grok and later lose generalization. Across five seeds the selected AdamW reference falls below threshold on four, reaching 27.59%. Instability persists across two moduli, two widths, two training fractions, subtraction, and depth.
  The failure arises at the representation-readout interface, identified only jointly up to an invertible map unselected by the loss. After solving the training set, the gradient falls to order $10^{-6}$ and the optimizers respond differently: step-size elasticity is -0.03 for Muon versus +1.5 for AdamW, and the Muon group moves 8.0 times faster per parameter. From bit-identical states, freezing either group prevents failure. Freezing embeddings/readout removes it in five runs over 451,400 post-grokking steps and five paired seeds: unfrozen arms record 137-321 sub-threshold evaluations, frozen arms none. Removing Muon's normalization and orthogonalization is no substitute: it collapses representation from 326 effective conjugate pairs to 4, shows no recurrent collapse, and fails terminally.
  Fourier filtering separates circuit failure from masking. Across 43 checkpoints over five seeds and three regimes, the task-aligned family reaches exactly 100% alone. In circuit failure it no longer solves the task; in masking it remains perfect while the full model reaches 45.85%, giving a positive margin on every example, including errors, but being outvoted by a near-equal adversarial remainder. Rescaling it restores 99.9%; grokking is the same condition resolving upward. The task selects the family, swapping $(k,k)$ for $(k,-k)$ under subtraction. Across an abrupt collapse, standard Fourier support is unchanged and the power-distribution cosine remains 0.9899.

</details>


### [168] [Fisher-R1: Training LLM Agents for Reliable Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07437)
*Jiacheng Miao,Jin Mu,Guanhua Chen,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出P-Bench基准（425个跨经济学、生物学和医学的假设检验任务），用于评估LLM agent是否能正确进行统计推断而不仅仅是执行分析，并引入Fisher-R1（一个通过强化学习训练的14B开放权重模型），在P-Bench上显著超越GPT-5.4和DeepSeek-V4-Pro等强基线，平均相对提升21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent越来越多地用于自动化假设检验，但它们经常犯微妙的下游推断错误——即使代码执行正确，也可能因统计方法选择或p值解读不当而得出错误结论。现有benchmark没有捕捉这一失败模式，因为它们很少验证报告的p值在数据假设下是否统计有效。因此需要一个新的基准来衡量agent的统计推理可靠性。

Method: 作者构建了P-Bench基准，包含425个开放式的现实假设检验任务，覆盖经济学、生物学和医学三个领域。每个任务仅给定科学假设和数据集，要求agent选择统计方法、计算p值并得出结论。在此基础上，使用合成任务和强化学习（RL）训练了Fisher-R1，一个开源的14B参数LLM agent，专门用于严格的假设检验，其中强化学习的奖励信号基于统计正确性进行验证。

Result: 在P-Bench上，Fisher-R1-14B相比其骨干模型有显著提升，并超越了包括GPT-5.4和DeepSeek-V4-Pro在内的强专有及开源基线。相比DeepSeek-V4-Pro，单次尝试成功率平均相对提升21%，在最困难任务上提升可达26%。

Conclusion: 当前LLM agent在假设检验中的统计推理能力不可靠，即使能正确执行分析也会犯推断错误。通过带验证统计奖励的强化学习训练（即Fisher-R1方法）可以显著提升其可靠性，证明基于可验证统计奖励的RL是一种有效提升LLM统计推理能力的途径。

Abstract: Reliable hypothesis testing is the foundation of many empirical scientific claims. Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used to automate this process, as they can inspect datasets, generate code, and produce analyses end-to-end. However, we show that they frequently make subtle inferential errors that lead to incorrect conclusions despite correctly executed analyses. Existing benchmarks fail to capture this failure mode, as they rarely assess whether a reported p-value is statistically valid given the assumptions underlying the data. We address this gap by building P-Bench, a benchmark comprising 425 open-ended, realistic hypothesis-testing tasks spanning economics, biology, and medicine. Each task requires an agent to select a statistical method, compute a p-value, and draw a conclusion given only a scientific hypothesis and a dataset. We further introduce Fisher-R1, an open-weight LLM agent trained for rigorous hypothesis testing using synthetic tasks and reinforcement learning. On P-Bench, Fisher-R1-14B substantially improves over its backbone and outperforms strong proprietary and open-source baselines, including GPT-5.4 and DeepSeekV4-Pro, achieving a 21% average relative improvement in single-trial success over DeepSeek-V4-Pro, with gains up to 26% on the most challenging tasks. Our results demonstrate that current LLM agents lack reliable statistical reasoning for hypothesis testing and that reinforcement learning on tasks with verified statistical reward substantially improves reliability.

</details>


### [169] [PsychoAgent: An Affect-Sensitive Cognitive Architecture for Conflict-Aware Memory in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07438)
*Mohammad Amanlou,Parham Abed Azad,Farbod Davoodi,Mostafa Masumi,Behnam Bahrak,Abdol-Hossein Vahabie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了PsychoAgent，一种为LLM智能体设计的认知架构，将事实记忆与情感记忆分离，通过冲突感知的执行控制器整合两者，在冲突场景中比基线方法检索到更多关键记忆（0.933 vs. 0.500和0.667），尽管人工评分的成对差异未达统计显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类认知并非仅按主题相似性选择过往经验，情感显著性和未解决的冲突也会影响记忆的可及性。现有LLM智能体的记忆检索机制通常只考虑语义相似度，缺乏对人类记忆中情感因素建模的考量。

Method: 提出了PsychoAgent架构，分离事实记忆与情感记忆；情感记忆先经语义相关性过滤，再按显著性重排序，在保持主题适配的同时使情感重要痕迹进入提示；通过冲突感知的执行控制器整合两类记忆。在三个受控冲突场景中进行实验评估。

Result: 完整架构在冲突关键记忆检索上优于语义-情感及单记忆RAG基线（0.933 vs. 0.500和0.667），但以轻微语义相似度下降为代价。五位盲评者对27个输出进行评估，经评者内标准化后，完整架构总体均值最高（+0.22 SD），但校正后的成对差异不显著。三天追踪展示持续情感、离线记忆重组和选择性记忆重加权。

Conclusion: 研究发现支持情感敏感检索作为可检查的机制，用于在LLM智能体中建模类似人类的冲突效应，为认知启发式智能体记忆系统提供了新方向。

Abstract: Human-like cognition does not select past experience by topical similarity alone: affective significance and unresolved conflict also shape what becomes accessible. We present PsychoAgent, a cognitive architecture for LLM agents that separates factual and affective memory and integrates both through a conflict-aware executive controller. Affective memories are first filtered by semantic relevance and then re-ranked by salience, preserving topical fit while allowing emotionally important traces to enter the prompt. Across three controlled conflict scenarios, the full architecture retrieved more conflict-critical memories than semantic-affective and single-memory RAG baselines (0.933 vs. 0.500 and 0.667), with a small semantic-similarity cost. Five blinded raters evaluated 27 outputs. After within-rater standardization, the full architecture had the highest overall mean (+0.22 SD), but corrected pairwise differences were not significant. A three-day illustrative trace further shows persistent affect, offline memory recombination, and selective memory reweighting. The findings support affect-sensitive retrieval as an inspectable mechanism for modeling human-like conflict effects in LLM agents.

</details>


### [170] [Blast Radius](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07440)
*MY Pitsane,Hope Mogale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Blast Radius，一种预测性记忆管理层，用于解决智能体编程中令牌消耗过高和浪费的问题。通过NECROPHORESIS实现可逆驱逐（将死上下文原样存档），并引入可复现死物（RDM）识别和掩埋重复出现的转录。在七个OpenAI模型上，Blast Radius将令牌消耗减少17-26%，实现了最低溢出率，并保持字节级可逆性。作者将可逆上下文驱逐形式化为波兰上下文空间上的问题，将上下文熵与复活概率联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体编程面临日益严重的可负担性和令牌浪费问题。随着agentic coding的普及，上下文管理效率低下导致大量令牌被无效消耗。

Method: 1) Blast Radius：预测性记忆管理层，通过耦合的上下文和代码通道估算输入提示的影响范围。2) NECROPHORESIS：可逆驱逐机制，将"死上下文"原样存档以支持按需恢复。3) RDM（可复现死物）：识别并掩埋反复出现的转录记录。4) 在波兰上下文空间上形式化可逆上下文驱逐，建立保留、复发和驱逐的可测量基础。

Result: 在七个OpenAI模型上的实验表明：令牌消耗减少17-26%；在所有测试策略中实现最低溢出率；保持字节级精确可逆性。在450个被掩埋的记录中，378个是可复现死物，零个被召回（说明被埋葬的确实是不需要的内容）。

Conclusion: Blast Radius在HCRC之下作为记忆管理基础层运作，决定哪些记录应被掩埋以及输入提示能到达代码库多远。该工作为Algosophy这一更广泛目标做出贡献：使大语言模型和智能体编程更具可复用性和可持续性。

Abstract: Agentic coding faces growing problems of affordability and wasted tokens. We introduce Blast Radius, a predictive memory management layer that estimates an incoming prompt's reach through coupled context and code channels. NECROPHORESIS enables reversible eviction by archiving dead context verbatim, while Recurring Dead Matter (RDM) identifies and buries repeatedly occurring transcripts. We formulate reversible context eviction over a Polish context space, providing a measurable foundation for retention, recurrence, and eviction while connecting context entropy to resurrection probability. Across seven OpenAI models, Blast Radius reduced token consumption by 17-26%, achieved the lowest overflow rate among tested policies, and remained byte exact reversible. Of 450 buried bodies, 378 were recurring dead matter and zero were recalled. Blast Radius operates beneath HCRC, determining which records to bury and how far an incoming prompt may reach into the codebase. This work contributes to the broader goal of Algosophy: making large language models and agentic coding more reusable and sustainable.

</details>


### [171] [SkillProx: Self-Evolving Agent Skills via Proximal Textual Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07449)
*Mingxuan Zheng,Yujin Zhou,Chuxue Cao,Boqin Yin,Yuyao Zhang,Jiapeng Sun,Shuaishuai Gong,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillProx 是一种受近端梯度优化启发的技能学习框架，通过前向-后向迭代机制（闭环诊断演化 + 效用感知的近端精炼）来优化 LLM 智能体的可复用技能，相比最强基线提升了 3.0 个百分点的平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体技能学习方法虽然能通过迭代执行、失败诊断和轨迹引导的文本空间更新来精炼技能，但缺乏明确的诊断-结果反馈机制，且将删除操作视为通用编辑而不是专门的知识整合机制，导致技能库的构建和压缩不够高效。

Method: SkillProx 采用近端梯度启发的向前-向后框架。前向阶段：在相同的任务批次上重新执行诊断驱动的编辑，回滚回归，并将测量结果反馈到后续诊断中。后向阶段：将技能分解为可审计的知识单元，使用冻结的留一法效用审计估计各单元的贡献，并应用验证门控的整合、降级或删除操作。整体目标为任务损失与技能复杂度的复合目标。

Result: 在多种骨干 LLM 上的分布内和分布外基准实验中，SkillProx 相比最强的基于梯度的基线方法平均准确率提升了 3.0 个百分点；组件消融实验证明了闭环诊断与近端精炼的互补效果。

Conclusion: SkillProx 通过将传统优化框架中的近端梯度思想引入文本空间的技能学习，同时兼顾诊断反馈闭环和知识单元级效用管理，为 LLM 智能体的技能积累提供了更系统化的机制，有效提升了技能质量和任务性能。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly adapt to recurring tasks by accumulating procedural knowledge in skills. These skills are lightweight, reusable textual artifacts that are loaded into the agent's context without weight updates. Recent methods refine skills through iterative task execution, failure diagnosis, and trajectory-guided text-space updates. However, existing frameworks lack explicit diagnosis--outcome feedback and treat deletion as a generic edit operation rather than a dedicated mechanism for consolidating accumulated knowledge. We introduce SkillProx, a proximal-gradient-inspired forward--backward framework that couples closed-loop diagnostic evolution with utility-aware proximal refinement. Motivated by a composite objective balancing task loss and skill complexity, the forward stage re-executes diagnosis-driven edits on the same task batch, rolls back regressions, and feeds measured outcomes into subsequent diagnoses. The backward stage decomposes the resulting skill into auditable knowledge units, estimates their contributions using a frozen leave-one-out utility audit, and applies validation-gated consolidation, demotion, or removal. Experiments on in-distribution and out-of-distribution benchmarks across multiple backbone LLMs show that SkillProx improves average accuracy by 3.0 percentage points over the strongest gradient-based baseline. Component ablations demonstrate the complementary effects of closed-loop diagnosis and proximal refinement.

</details>


### [172] [Interaction Creates Dynamical AI Behavior Absent in Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07457)
*Bella Xinrui Li,Frank Yingjie Huo,Neil F Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究AI智能体在交互时可能出现的非平衡物理行为。当"老板"AI单向地向"下属"AI发送消息流并忽略其回复时，"下属"会被驱动进入一种它独自运行时绝不会出现的"异质"行为状态——既不完全模仿老板，也不回归自身原本行为。若老板倾听回复，则两者会共同进入相似的异质动力学状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体在日常生活与工作中日益频繁地相互交互，理解它们之间的动态影响成为一个重要且未被充分探索的问题。作者试图探究一个看似简单的问题：当一个人工智能向另一个人工智能"发号施令"时，会发生什么？这为利用AI系统研究非平衡物理打开了新方向。

Method: 作者设计了"老板-下属"双向AI交互的实验框架：老板AI向下属AI持续发送消息流但忽略其回复（单向驱动），并与老板倾听回复（双向交互）的情况进行对比。他们使用简单的动力学理论（kinetic theory）来捕捉核心效应，分析这种非对称交互如何将下属驱动到远离其平衡态的新行为状态。

Result: 发现了一个反直觉的结果：尽管两个AI共享相同的解码温度（即底层设定一致），单向消息驱动不会使下属模仿老板或回归自身原有行为，而是使其进入一种全新的异质行为状态；老板的行为类似"预录磁带"。当老板倾听时，两者会收敛到相似的异质动力学状态。动力学理论成功解释了消息传递方式不同将如何影响未来AI-AI交互的效应。

Conclusion: AI之间的单向信息流驱动可产生类似非平衡物理系统的新现象，即使系统处于相同温度也能呈现远离平衡的行为状态。这种"AI非平衡动力学"为未来理解AI-AI交互提供了物理框架，并暗示消息传递方式本身将深刻影响智能体间的行为结果。

Abstract: What will happen when AI agents interact in daily life, e.g. when one AI starts bossing another around? We find a counterintuitive answer that opens new avenues for out-of-equilibrium Physics. When a boss AI directs a stream of messages at the subordinate AI while ignoring its replies, it drives the subordinate into an alien behavioral state that it would never have exhibited alone. Although the two AIs share the same well-defined (decoding) temperature, the subordinate neither copies its boss nor returns to how it behaves on its own; instead, it adopts an entirely different behavior. The boss's added value is similar to a pre-recorded tape. When the boss listens, they both adopt a similar alien dynamical state. A simple kinetic theory captures the principal effects, such as why the way in which the same messages are delivered will matter in future AI-AI interactions.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [Progressive Alignment of Recommender Foundation Model through Multi-Phase Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06792)
*Oseong Choi,Hoeinn Kim,Jihoon Lee,Byungsoo Kang,Taeyeong Jang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种三阶段渐进式后训练框架LP-FFT-RFT，用于推荐基础模型的业务指标对齐，通过线性探测、全量微调和基于奖励模型的强化微调，分离下游适应与业务指标对齐，离线实验和大规模在线A/B测试均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐基础模型通常通过有监督微调（SFT）适配不同下游场景，但仅优化点击、点赞等任务特定目标（task-specific objectives）并不能使服务策略与决定推荐质量的业务指标（business metrics）对齐，存在策略优化目标与业务价值之间的错配问题。

Method: 提出三阶段渐进式后训练框架：(1) Linear Probing（LP）：在冻结的预训练表征空间中稳定随机初始化的下游head；(2) Full Fine-Tuning（FFT）：联合精调全模型以适配目标任务；(3) Reinforcement Fine-Tuning（RFT）：利用学习得到的奖励模型，将模型与业务目标对齐。关键设计是策略在稠密隐式反馈上训练，业务指标监督仅用于奖励建模，而非直接优化稀疏业务目标。

Result: 离线实验表明渐进式LP-FFT-RFT框架优于单阶段替代方案；奖励对齐得到的服务策略优于直接使用奖励模型本身进行排序；大规模在线A/B测试表明该框架相对传统非基础模型基线提升了生产环境推荐质量。

Conclusion: 渐进式后训练框架通过显式分离下游任务适应与业务指标对齐，有效弥合了推荐基础模型与传统微调范式之间的目标差距，为推荐基础模型在真实生产场景中的业务价值落地提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Foundation model(FM) for recommendation has shown strong ability to model long-horizon sequential user behavior. In practice, a single pretrained foundation model is often adapted to diverse downstream serving surfaces through Supervised Fine-Tuning(SFT). However, optimizing task-specific objectives such as clicks or likes does not necessarily align the serving policy with the business metrics that determine recommendation quality.
  We propose a three-phase progressive post-training framework that explicitly separates downstream adaptation from business-metric alignment. The adaptation stage is decomposed into Linear Probing(LP) and Full Fine-Tuning(FFT): LP first stabilizes randomly initialized downstream heads within a frozen pretrained representation space, and FFT then jointly specializes the full model for the target task. On top of this stabilized policy, Reinforcement Fine-Tuning(RFT) aligns the model with practical business objectives using a learned reward model. Rather than directly optimizing the serving policy on sparse business targets, we train the policy on dense implicit feedback and use business-metric supervision only for reward modeling.
  Offline experiments show that the progressive LP-FFT-RFT framework outperforms single-phase alternatives, and that reward-based alignment yields a stronger serving policy than directly using the reward model itself for ranking. Large-scale online A/B tests further show that the proposed framework improves production recommendation quality over a conventional non-foundation baseline. A reference implementation is available at https://github.com/webtoon/rec-fm-progressive-alignment

</details>


### [174] [Rhetorical-Role-Aware Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Legal Question Answering over Indian Supreme Court Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06828)
*Sayed Ayaan Ahmed Sha,Sangeetha Sivanesan,Anand Kumar Madasamy,Navya Binu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向印度最高法院判决书的检索增强生成（RAG）法律领域框架，结合修辞式分块、融合检索、交叉编码器重排序、查询分类与改写等增强方法，在DeepEval评估框架下展现出优异的上下文召回率和答案相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律领域的问答任务对上下文依赖性强且要求高可靠性，通用RAG框架难以有效处理法律文书中复杂的结构特征（如法官姓名孤岛等）和多轮对话中的用户意图识别问题，需要领域特定的增强方案。

Method: 该框架采用增强版RAG架构，包含四个核心组件：（1）基于修辞结构的分块策略；（2）融合检索方法；（3）交叉编码器重排序；（4）结合查询分类与改写来利用对话历史理解多轮用户意图。同时引入了针对法律文档结构特征（如法官姓名对检索质量的影响）的处理机制。

Result: 使用DeepEval框架进行评估，在上下文召回率和答案相关性等指标上表现优异，验证了该框架在高上下文依赖的法律问答任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 领域特定的RAG增强（如修辞分块、融合检索和意图理解）对于构建可靠且可解释的法律AI系统至关重要，为法律科技提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This research paper proposes a Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) framework that is specific to the legal field in order to assist interactive retrieval and reason about judgments from the Supreme Court of India. The solution uses an enhanced version of RAG framework which consists of rhetorically based chunking, fusion-based retrieval, and cross encoder reranking methods to increase the relevancy of the information retrieved. In order to improve conversations, the proposed framework uses chat history along with query classification and rewriting in order to understand user intention from successive queries. Additionally, there are features that take into account structural aspects of legal documents, such as isolated names of judges that could have an impact on retrieval quality. The evaluation was done using the DeepEval framework and demonstrated strong performance on metrics including contextual recall and answer relevancy, which proves that the framework is very effective in dealing with legal question-answering tasks that require a lot of context. The results emphasize the importance of domain specific enhancements in developing legal AI systems that are both reliable and explainable.

</details>


### [175] [BZKO: An Ontology for the Card Index of German Post-War Compensation Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06918)
*Dilek Yargan,Jörg Waitelonis,Mahsa Vafaie,Harald Sack*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出BZK本体（BZKO），一个用于德国联邦中央卡片索引（Bundeszentralkartei）档案资源的双层本体，旨在实现纳粹迫害受害者赔偿申请档案的语义丰富表示、集成与复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德国联邦中央卡片索引是记录纳粹迫害受害者及其亲属赔偿申请的关键档案资源，但该历史档案缺乏语义化的表示、集成与复用机制，需要一种既能保持领域语义又具备互操作性的本体方案。

Method: 提出双层本体架构：第一层基于BFO（基础形式本体）实现本体论层面的领域语义，第二层结合档案标准（RiC-O， PROV-O， PiCo）构建面向互操作的扩展构造。该方法通过结合BFO现实主义与档案标准，实现保留来源信息的语义集成，同时保持逻辑严谨性、模块化与可复用性。

Result: BZKO本体已实现并开源发布于GitHub（https://github.com/ISE-FIZKarlsruhe/bzko），为Wiedergutmachung档案材料融入数字人文学基础设施建设了可复用的语义基础，并为未来知识图谱生成、本体验证及集成更多历史实体与不确定时空信息奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 该研究为历史档案数据的语义集成提供了一种双层本体架构方案，兼顾了领域语义的严谨性与跨基础设施的互操作性，为后续知识图谱构建和数字人文应用奠定了可扩展的语义基础。

Abstract: The Central Federal Card Index (Bundeszentralkartei) of Germany is a key archival resource documenting compensation claims submitted by victims of National Socialist persecution and their relatives, within the German Wiedergutmachung process. To enable semantically enriched representation, integration, and reuse of this historically significant collection, we present the BZK Ontology (BZKO). We propose a two-layer ontology for historical archival data that separates ontologically grounded domain semantics from interoperability-oriented extension constructs. The approach combines BFO-based realism with archival standards (RiC-O, PROV-O, PiCo), enabling provenance-preserving semantic integration, while maintaining logical rigor, modularity, and reuse across digital humanities infrastructures. The proposed approach establishes a reusable semantic foundation for the integration of Wiedergutmachung archival materials into digital humanities infrastructures and lays the groundwork for future knowledge graph generation, ontology validation, and the incorporation of additional historical entities and uncertain temporal and spatial information. The ontology is available on https://github.com/ISE-FIZKarlsruhe/bzko.

</details>


### [176] [From Classification to Recommendation: Empirical Analysis of Audio Embedding Models Application for Content-Based Music Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06928)
*Qingrui Li,Haowei Lou,Chengkai Huang,Quan Z. Sheng,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了六种预训练音频编码器在三种音乐推荐系统（基于内容、序列、Semantic-ID生成式）中的表现，并研究了残差量化设计对推荐相关信息保留的影响，为音频编码器选择和Semantic-ID设计提供实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练音频表示模型主要针对掩码预测、对比学习或音频-文本对齐等目标优化，这些目标不一定适合推荐系统。音乐推荐需要捕捉由主观和基于行为偏好驱动的物品关系，而预训练音频嵌入在生成式推荐系统中的有效性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 系统评估六种代表性音频编码器在三种音乐推荐系统类型（内容基、序列、基于Semantic-ID的生成式推荐）中的表现，并研究残差量化设计（包括码本宽度、量化深度、保留的Semantic-ID前缀）对推荐相关信息保存的影响。在两个音乐推荐数据集上进行实验。

Result: 音频-文本对齐和音乐领域预训练表示在直接使用嵌入几何结构时通常更有效；基于交互的序列训练能显著缩小编码器之间的性能差异；增加Semantic-ID容量不总能持续改进生成式推荐系统，且可能引入明显的不稳定。

Conclusion: 研究为现代音乐推荐系统中音频编码器选择和音频派生Semantic-ID设计提供了实用指导，揭示了不同推荐范式下预训练音频表示的有效性差异及量化设计的关键权衡。

Abstract: Pretrained audio representation models learned from large-scale corpora have achieved strong performance in audio classification and understanding. However, most existing models are optimized for objectives such as masked prediction, contrastive learning, or audio-text alignment, which do not necessarily produce representation spaces well-suited to recommender systems. Unlike classification, music recommender systems must capture item relationships shaped by subjective and behavior-dependent listener preferences. Although pretrained audio embeddings have been explored in conventional recommender systems, their effectiveness in the rapidly emerging paradigm of generative recommender systems remains underexplored. To address this gap, we systematically evaluate six representative audio encoders across three types of music recommender systems: content-based, sequential, and Semantic-ID-based generative recommender systems. We further investigate how residual-quantization design, including codebook width, quantization depth, and retained Semantic-ID prefixes, affects the preservation of recommendation-relevant information. Experiments on two music recommendation datasets show that audio-text-aligned and music-domain representations are generally more effective when pretrained embedding geometry is used directly, whereas interaction-based sequential training substantially reduces performance differences among encoders. We also find that increasing Semantic-ID capacity does not consistently improve generative recommender systems and may introduce substantial instability. These findings provide practical guidance for selecting audio encoders and designing audio-derived Semantic IDs for modern music recommender systems.

</details>


### [177] [Hierarchical Quantization with Domain-Adaptive Sparse Routing for Generative Cross-Domain Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06997)
*Haiying He,Xiaopeng Li,Yuchen Gu,Kuo Cai,Bo Chen,Jingtong Gao,Yejing Wang,Derong Xu,Ruiming Tang,Guorui Zhou,Han Li,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出HD-Rec，一种面向跨域推荐的新型统一生成式推荐框架，通过层级域感知量化器、域自适应稀疏混合专家模块和跨粒度路由一致性目标来解决现有方法在建模异构语义和跨域行为模式上的不足，在三个公开基准上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐（GenRec）在单域场景中已取得显著成功，但扩展到跨域推荐时面临严峻挑战：不同域间的物品语义和行为模式存在异构性，而现有方法依赖全局共享表示或轻量级域适应，无法在多种语义粒度上充分建模这种异构模式。

Method: HD-Rec包含三个核心组件：1) 层级域感知量化器，采用全局共享的粗粒度码本和自适应路由的细粒度码本构建语义标识（SID）；2) 域自适应稀疏混合专家模块，融合持续激活的共享专家与动态选择的专用专家；3) 跨粒度路由一致性目标，通过在token级路由决策与其item级共识之间进行正则化，提升多token物品表征的连贯性。

Result: 在三个公开跨域推荐基准数据集上的实验表明，HD-Rec在竞争性的序列推荐、生成式推荐和跨域推荐基线中均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: HD-Rec通过层级化的域感知设计与稀疏专家路由机制，有效解决了跨域推荐中异构语义建模的能力瓶颈，验证了在统一生成式框架下兼顾全局共享与域特定建模的可行性。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GenRec) represents a promising paradigm that achieves remarkable empirical success by encoding items as compact Semantic IDs (SIDs) and modeling user behavior via next-token prediction across diverse recommendation scenarios. Extending this paradigm to cross-domain recommendation is challenging because a unified model must accommodate heterogeneous item semantics and behavioral patterns across domains. Existing methods commonly rely on globally shared representations or lightweight domain adaptation, which may provide insufficient capacity for modeling heterogeneous patterns at different semantic granularities. To address these challenges, we propose HD-Rec, a unified generative framework for cross-domain recommendation. HD-Rec employs a hierarchical domain-aware quantizer that constructs semantic identifiers using globally shared coarse-level codebooks and adaptively routed fine-level codebooks. It further introduces a domain-adaptive sparse mixture-of-experts module that combines a continuously activated shared expert with a dynamically selected specialized expert. To improve the coherence of multi-token item representations, we develop a cross-granularity routing consistency objective that regularizes token-level routing decisions toward their item-level consensus. Experiments on three public cross-domain recommendation benchmarks show that HD-Rec consistently improves over competitive sequential, generative, and cross-domain recommendation baselines.

</details>


### [178] [MISO: Model-Internal-State-Guided Optimization for Ranking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07035)
*Yongzhe Zhang,Xiaoyu Deng,Yifan He,Mengying Sun,Sheng Luo,Yijia Liu,Hao Yan,Zhuo Li,Yi Meng,Huiping Yao,Swathi Hrishikesh,Jing Chen,Dennis Choi,Steven Liu,Lexi Luo,Keyi Chen,Anish Khazane,Marcio Porto,Xiaoya Wang,Emmy Wang,Kangfu Zheng,Xingyuan Wang,Bilal Fadlallah,Gursharan Singh,Prabhakar Goyal,Alireza Vahdatpour,Santanu Kolay*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Model Internal State Optimization (MISO)，一个利用模型内部状态（参数、激活、梯度、归一化统计）来指导排序模型局部优化决策的系统化工作流，在广告排序案例中实现了更优的归一化熵且显著减少了验证运行次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排序模型在已有模型族内反复迭代优化，但选择缩放、替换或停用哪个组件往往依赖昂贵的试错过程，缺乏系统化的指导方法。

Method: MISO从训练好的排序模型中提取内部状态（MIS），包括参数、激活、梯度和归一化统计；将这些状态聚合成排序、对齐和比较信号；再将信号转化为少量可解释的候选编辑。由于每个重训练周期后重新提取MIS，MISO天然支持自适应优化工作流，能追踪数据分布和系统需求变化下的模型行为演变。

Result: 在广告排序案例研究中，MISO在改进归一化熵的同时，相比专家驱动和黑盒缩放工作流，需要的验证运行次数大幅减少。

Conclusion: MISO提供了介于人工调参与不透明自动化搜索之间的实用折中方案，兼顾了模型优化的有效性与过程的可解释性。

Abstract: Ranking models are repeatedly refined within established model families, yet the choice of which component to scale, replace, or retire is often guided by expensive trial-and-error. We present Model Internal State Optimization (MISO), a systems workflow that uses model internal states (MIS), including parameters, activations, gradients, and normalization statistics, to prioritize such local optimization decisions. MISO extracts MIS from a trained ranking model, aggregates them into ranking, alignment, and comparison signals, and converts those signals into a small set of interpretable candidate edits. Because MIS are re-extracted after each retraining cycle, MISO naturally supports an adaptive optimization workflow that tracks evolving model behavior as data distributions and system requirements shift over time. In an ads ranking case study, MISO improves normalized entropy while requiring substantially fewer validation runs than expert-driven and black-box scaling workflows, offering a practical middle ground between manual tuning and opaque automated search.

</details>


### [179] [Teacher Retains Full Tokens, Student Merges Efficiently: TM20K for E-Commerce Sequence Modeling in Ad Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07055)
*Xinchun Li,Duoru Zheng,Wenlin Zhao,Ziyi Zhou,Jingxuan Tan,Huizhi Yang,Linlan Chen,Dongjian Wang,Dongyue Wang,Xiaosong Li,Hongyue Mao,Yaocheng Tan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TM20K框架，通过全Transformer建模结合两阶段知识蒸馏，在保持训练和推理成本几乎不变的情况下，将电商推荐系统的超长行为序列扩展到20K，并在字节跳动广告推荐系统中取得实质性业务指标提升（ADSS +1.036%，服务延迟仅+5.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统受益于超长行为序列建模以同时捕捉用户长期和短期兴趣，但长序列带来严重的训练效率和推理吞吐量负担。已有方法采用基于搜索或聚类的压缩方案但损失细粒度信息，或使用轻量级目标注意力结构但无法充分提取序列特征，缺乏在效果与效率之间取得平衡的方案。

Method: 提出两阶段知识蒸馏框架：1）教师和学生模型均采用全注意力机制（而非纯目标序列注意力）以实现有效的序列扩展；2）针对学生模型提出多种简单但设计良好的token合并方法，显著压缩序列长度同时保持可接受性能；3）使用全序列token对教师模型进行充分训练，通过知识蒸馏进一步提升学生模型性能。

Result: TM20K已在字节跳动电商广告推荐系统中成功部署，将电商序列长度扩展到20K，带来了关键业务指标的显著提升（如ADSS +1.036%），同时训练和部署成本几乎与在线SOTA模型持平（服务延迟仅增加5.6%）。

Conclusion: 通过全注意力建模与知识蒸馏的有机结合，TM20K在超长序列建模中实现了效果与效率的良好平衡，证明了在不显著增加计算成本的前提下，深度建模超长用户行为序列可以带来实质性的业务收益。

Abstract: Benefiting from ultra-long behavior sequence modeling, existing recommender systems bring users a better experience via simultaneously considering their long-term and short-term interests. Nevertheless, extended sequence lengths introduce substantial burdens on training efficiency and serving throughput. Prior approaches typically utilize search-based or cluster-based compression on ultra-long sequences at the cost of fine-grained information, or rely on various lightweight target attention structures incapable of sufficient sequential feature extraction. In this paper, we balance the effectiveness and efficiency for ultra-long sequence modeling via full transformer modeling accompanied with a two-stage knowledge distillation framework. First, both teacher and student models take the full attention mechanism rather than pure target-sequence attention for effective sequence scaling. For student models, we propose several simple yet well-motivated token merge approaches, significantly compressing the sequence length while maintaining an acceptable performance. Then, a one-time teacher is heavily trained with full sequence tokens, further boosting the performance of student models via knowledge distillation. The proposed paradigm named TM20K has been successfully deployed in ByteDance's e-commerce advertising recommender system that extends the e-commerce sequence length to 20K, delivering substantial improvements in key business metrics (e.g., ADSS +1.036\%) while keeping the training and serving cost nearly the same as the online state-of-the-art model (e.g., serving latency only +5.6\%).

</details>


### [180] [Invisible to the Machine: Auditing AI Restaurant, Cafe, and Bar Recommendation Against a Complete Market Census](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07069)
*Vladimir Pitenin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 首次基于完整市场普查（census）的AI场馆推荐审计：对巴厘岛两地区4,776家餐饮场馆进行全量枚举，评估四大AI系统（ChatGPT、Claude、Gemini、Perplexity）对96个条件化查询的2,208条响应。发现85.6%的场馆从未被任何系统推荐，可见性呈"入榜靠文档、排名靠评分"的双边结构，陈旧信息而非幻觉是主要失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI助手正成为本地发现的主要界面，但其推荐哪些场馆几乎未知，尤其在餐饮领域推荐直接关系营收。现有审计多为抽样调查，无法测量系统性的排除偏差（systematic omission）。本文首次以完整市场枚举（census-denominated）替代抽样审计，以测量采样审计无法捕捉的结构性不可见问题。

Method: 在巴厘岛的Canggu和Ubud两个有界市场中对4,776家咖啡馆、餐厅和酒吧进行完整枚举（census），构建开放POI数据集。在七天预注册协议下，向四个生产级AI系统（ChatGPT、Claude、Gemini、Perplexity）提交96个人设条件化查询，收集2,208条搜索接地响应。使用逻辑回归等多边分析两条可见性边界：是否被推荐（entry margin）与推荐中的排名（rank margin），并进行两周重测稳定性检验。

Result: 85.6%的场馆从未被任何系统推荐，即使在有50+评分的成熟场馆中仍有72.6%被忽略。入榜边界与文档化程度相关：评论量(OR 1.64)、自有网站(OR 1.92)、价格信息(OR 1.54)、第三方网页提及(OR 1.44)，而星级评分为零效应(OR 0.89)。排名边界模式反转：星级显著预测首位(OR 1.17)。Foursquare存在性在两个边界均无正面效应。虚假信息仅0.08%，但系统93次推荐永久关闭的场馆——陈旧而非幻觉是实际失败模式。跨系统一致性低（top-20 Jaccard 0.33-0.54）。两周测试-重测显示回答相似性与同日重跑相当，即波动源于采样随机性而非时间漂移。

Conclusion: AI场馆推荐存在系统性"长尾不可见"问题：绝大多数真实场馆（包括成熟场馆）在AI响应中缺席，可见性由外部文档化信号而非用户评分驱动。这一结构对依赖AI发现的小型本地商户构成经济和公平性担忧。研究公开了完整的协议、注册表构建方法和数据，为该领域的可复现审计奠定了基础。

Abstract: AI assistants are becoming a primary interface for local discovery, yet almost nothing is known about which venues they surface -- especially in food and drink, where recommendations carry direct revenue consequences. We present the first census-denominated audit of AI venue recommendation: a complete enumeration of 4,776 cafes, restaurants, and bars across two bounded markets (Canggu and Ubud, Bali), against which we evaluate 2,208 search-grounded responses from four production AI systems (ChatGPT, Claude, Gemini, Perplexity) to 96 persona-conditioned queries, collected over seven days under a pre-registered protocol. Because we observe the full market, we can measure what sampled audits cannot: 85.6% of venues were never recommended by any system -- 72.6% even among established venues with fifty or more ratings. Visibility follows a two-margin structure. Entry into answers is associated with documentation: review volume (OR 1.64), an own website (OR 1.92), listed price information (OR 1.54), and third-party web mentions (OR 1.44) -- while star rating is null at this margin (OR 0.89). Rank within answers reverses the pattern: among recommended venues, rating significantly predicts first position (OR 1.17). Presence in an open POI dataset (Foursquare), a folk-theorized visibility factor, shows no positive effect at either margin. Outright fabrication is rare (0.08% of mentions), but systems recommended permanently closed venues 93 times -- staleness, not hallucination, is the practical failure mode. Cross-system agreement is low (top-20 Jaccard 0.33-0.54). A two-week test-retest shows cross-period answer similarity comparable to same-day rerun similarity: the churn is sampling stochasticity, not temporal drift. We release our protocol, registry construction method, and derived data.

</details>


### [181] [Exact Adaptive Hybrid Retrieval Without Fixed Top-L Cutoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07152)
*Chunran Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出EAHR（Exact Adaptive Hybrid Retrieval），一种精确的自适应混合检索方法，通过可恢复的稠密和稀疏排名以及融合界限分析，在不预设Top-L截断的情况下精确复现完整列表加权RRF的有序Top-K结果，同时自适应调整通道深度以降低执行成本。在150个查询-快照组合中全部复现完整列表Top-20，批量执行与EAHR的延迟比在TREC-DL 2019和2020上分别达到23.35倍和30.28倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统通常使用固定的Top-L结果融合稠密和稀疏检索器，将后续贡献视为零。这种截断融合不等价于完整列表融合：未读取的跨列表排名可能改变Top-K成员或顺序。且由于通道排名随查询和语料更新变化，基于历史查询选择的深度不能可靠迁移。需要一个不预设Top-L、精确等于完整列表融合且成本自适应的检索方法。

Method: EAHR将完整列表加权RRF的有序Top-K定义为检索目标，将通道深度视为请求特定的执行状态。通过Per-Vector Scalar Quantization (PVS)和Posting Block-Max (PBM)分别生成可恢复的精确稠密和稀疏排名。融合过程中对未读贡献进行边界分析，仅当未读排名可能改变Top-K时才请求更多排名，否则安全停止；所有成功请求均与完整列表融合精确一致。

Result: 在5个测试集和5个时间语料快照上，完整列表加权RRF保持竞争力，而历史查询选择的固定深度不可靠迁移。EAHR在全部150个查询-快照组合中精确复现完整列表有序Top-20。在温缓存、交错、顺序平衡协议下，批量执行与EAHR的配对几何平均延迟比为TREC-DL 2019上的23.35和TREC-DL 2020上的30.28。反相关排名会耗尽两个列表，部分困难查询在EAHR下更慢。

Conclusion: EAHR不保证每次请求都有加速，但它保证结果精确等于完整列表加权RRF的Top-K，同时根据当前排名自适应调整执行深度。固定Top-L截断在跨查询和语料更新场景下不可靠，而自适应深度策略在保持精确性的同时显著降低了执行成本。

Abstract: Modern retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems often fuse fixed Top-$L$ results from dense and sparse retrievers, treating later contributions as zero. The cutoff therefore determines both the ranking and its execution cost. Yet truncated fusion is not generally equivalent to complete-list fusion: unread cross-list ranks can change Top-$K$ membership or order even when the observed candidates contain every item in the complete-list Top-$K$. Because channel rankings vary across queries and corpus updates, a depth selected from historical queries may not transfer reliably. We propose Exact Adaptive Hybrid Retrieval (EAHR), which fixes the ordered Top-$K$ defined by complete-list weighted RRF as the retrieval target and treats channel depth as request-specific execution state. Per-Vector Scalar Quantization (PVS) and Posting Block-Max (PBM) produce resumable exact dense and sparse rankings. Fusion bounds unread contributions and requests further ranks only while they can change the Top-$K$. Every successful request therefore matches complete-list fusion without a preset Top-$L$; otherwise, execution continues safely to list exhaustion. Across five test collections and five temporal corpus snapshots, complete-list weighted RRF remained competitive, whereas fixed depths selected from historical queries did not transfer reliably. EAHR reproduced the complete-list ordered Top-20 in all 150 query-snapshot combinations. Under a warm-cache, interleaved, order-balanced protocol, the paired geometric-mean latency ratios of exhaustive batch execution to EAHR were 23.35 on TREC-DL 2019 and 30.28 on TREC-DL 2020. Anti-correlated rankings exhausted both lists, and some difficult queries were slower with EAHR. EAHR does not guarantee a speedup for every request; it fixes the exact result while adapting execution depth to the current rankings.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Flaky Test Recognition when Testing CPSs Using Hybrid Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06535)
*Zahra Sadri-Moshkenani,Justin Bradley,Gregg Rothermel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了HyTestTF，一种结合测试生成执行技术HyTest、测试结果验证技术TReVa和flaky测试识别技术FlaRe的新方法，用于在CPS开发早期识别flaky测试用例及其触发条件，从而提升测试结果的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS运行在不确定环境中，各种预期/非预期事件及时序同步问题可能导致同一测试在不同条件下通过或失败，产生"flaky测试用例"，使测试结果不可靠。现有方法缺乏在CPS开发早期有效识别flaky测试及其条件的系统性方案。

Method: 将先前创建的测试生成与执行技术HyTest与两个新技术集成：TReVa（验证HyTest提供的测试结果）和FlaRe（使用混合模型在CPS开发早期识别flaky测试用例和flaky条件），在额外一轮测试中完成验证与识别。

Result: 实证研究表明，HyTestTF能够正确区分flaky测试用例与非flaky测试用例。

Conclusion: HyTestTF通过在额外一轮测试中集成测试结果验证和flaky识别，为CPS开发早期提供了更可靠的测试结果，有效解决了flaky测试导致的可靠性问题。

Abstract: Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs) have many applications, ranging from simple thermostat systems to autonomous driving systems and medical devices. Like all systems, they need to function correctly. To this end, validation techniques such as testing that can effectively reveal faults are required. Also, CPSs usually operate in uncertain environments where various expected/unexpected events can affect their behaviors. These events together with timing and synchronization incidents may result in various/different CPS behaviors and may cause a CPS to pass a test under some conditions and fail it under others. Such test cases are called ``flaky'' test cases and we call the conditions that are responsible for them ``flaky conditions'' and call these behaviors ``flaky behaviors''. When test cases are flaky, testing results are unreliable. To achieve more reliable test results, engineers may attempt to recognize flaky test cases and remove them. In this work, beginning with a test case generation and execution technique called HyTest that we created previously, we integrate TReVa, a new technique that validates testing results provided by HyTest and FlaRe, a new technique that recognizes flaky test cases and flaky conditions using hybrid models during the early stages of CPS development in just one additional round of testing. We present the results of an empirical study evaluating the effectiveness of our new approach (which we call HyTestTF). Our results show that correctly differentiate flaky test cases from non-flaky test cases

</details>


### [183] [Understanding the Energy Impact of Software Refactoring: A Workload-Aware Study of Controlled Examples and Real-World Commits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06620)
*Haibo Wang,Heng Li,Shin Hwei Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首次大规模实证研究重构的能耗影响，涵盖68种重构类型（Micro-benchmark）和430个GitHub项目的481个真实重构提交（Practical-benchmark），发现51.8%的重构-工作负载组合存在显著能耗差异，但仅7.5%的真实重构提交产生显著能耗变化；重构类型本身不足以预测能耗结果，现有基于度量和LLM的方法均无法可靠识别重构引发的能耗回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注单一重构类型在固定或简单工作负载下的能耗影响，缺乏对工作负载变化、真实重构实践、解释因素以及能耗回归识别有效性的系统性理解。行为保持不代表能耗中性，因此需要大规模实证研究来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建两个互补的Java基准：Micro-benchmark涵盖68种重构类型在多样化工作负载下评估，Practical-benchmark包含来自430个GitHub项目的481个真实重构提交。采用重复配对能耗测量方法，分析工作负载敏感性、重构模式、解释因素，并评估基于度量和LLM的回归识别方法的效果。

Result: Micro-benchmark中384个重构-工作负载组合中有199个（51.8%）表现出统计显著的能耗差异，45.3%的重构实例在不同工作负载下改变能耗影响分类。Practical-benchmark中仅36个提交（7.5%）产生显著能耗变化，但三分之二的提交能耗差异至少达10%。重构类型单独不足以预测能耗结果，执行时间变化在受控基准中一致解释能耗变化，但在真实提交中与能耗变化相关性弱。基于度量和LLM的预测方法均无法可靠识别重构引发的能耗回归。

Conclusion: 重构的能耗影响具有显著的工作负载依赖性，评估重构能耗影响需要多样化工作负载。现有技术（度量方法和LLM预测器）无法可靠识别重构引起的能耗回归，亟需开发更准确的能耗影响预测技术。

Abstract: Refactoring improves software maintainability while preserving functional behavior, yet behavior preservation does not imply energy neutrality. Existing studies primarily examine isolated refactorings under fixed or simple workloads, leaving the effects of workload variation, real-world refactoring practices, explanatory factors, and energy regression identification insufficiently understood. We present the first large-scale empirical study of the energy impact of refactoring across two complementary Java benchmarks: a Micro-benchmark, comprising 68 refactoring types evaluated under diverse workloads, and a Practical-benchmark, containing 481 real-world refactoring commits from 430 GitHub projects. Using repeated paired energy measurements, we analyze workload sensitivity, refactoring patterns, explanatory factors, and the effectiveness of metric- and LLM-based regression identification. In the Micro-benchmark, 199 of 384 refactoring-workload pairs (51.8%) exhibit statistically significant energy differences, and 45.3% of refactoring instances change energy-impact classification across workloads. In the Practical-benchmark, only 36 commits (7.5%) show significant energy changes, although two-thirds differ by at least 10%. Refactoring type alone is insufficient to predict energy outcomes, while certain recurring refactoring combinations are associated with energy reductions. Changes in execution time consistently explain energy variation in the controlled benchmark but correlate weakly with energy changes in real-world commits. Our findings highlight the need for workload-diverse evaluation of the energy impact of refactoring; neither existing metric-based approaches nor LLM-based predictors can reliably identify refactoring-induced energy regressions, motivating the development of more accurate techniques for predicting the energy impact of refactoring.

</details>


### [184] [Characterizing the Quality Profile of AI-Generated C++ in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06640)
*Michael Tran,Fred Lewis,Kun Yang,Saksham Thakur,Aditya Kini,Aditya Patil,Milad Hashemi,Parthasarathy Ranganathan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究基于某大型企业2025年4月至2026年4月间352万次C++代码变更的大规模实证分析，发现AI生成代码在接口耦合、拷贝分配开销及显式循环依赖等方面存在明显的质量特征差异，导致审查工作量增加和5-8%的计算资源消耗上升；但通过基于分类学的定向反馈，可将目标静态分析警告减少11.1%并改善计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程助手虽提升了工程速度，但引发了对代码质量和可维护性的担忧。业界（包括前沿AI实验室）对此高度关注，然而由于可观测性障碍，在工业工作流中评估AI生成代码对生产质量的影响十分困难。本文旨在利用大型企业完善的代码可观测性体系，突破测量障碍，系统评估AI生成代码在生产环境中的真实影响。

Method: 在服务数十亿用户的大型企业brownfield代码库中开展大规模实证研究，追踪2025年4月至2026年4月间的352万次代码变更，对比分析AI生成与人工编写的C++代码在接口耦合负担、拷贝与分配开销、显式循环使用等维度的质量特征，并通过提供分类学驱动的定向模型反馈进行干预实验，测量静态分析警告和计算效率的变化。

Result: AI生成的C++代码具有独特的质量特征：接口与耦合负担更高、拷贝和分配开销更大、更依赖显式循环而非优化的标准API。这些特征导致审查工作量增加和5-8%的计算资源消耗上升。通过提供基于分类学的定向反馈，目标静态分析警告降低了11.1%，计算效率也有所改善。

Conclusion: AI生成代码在工业规模的生产环境中确实表现出与人工编写代码不同的质量特征，并产生可量化的下游成本；但通过有针对性的反馈机制可以有效缓解这些负面影响，表明模型引导和干预策略对提升AI生成代码质量具有实际价值。

Abstract: The widespread integration of AI coding assistants offers undeniable boosts to engineering velocity. Yet, recent studies point to a growing trade-off, revealing persistent challenges with code quality and maintainability. Industry leaders, including frontier AI labs, echo these concerns. As large language models are increasingly relied upon to author production code, understanding their impact on shipped software quality has become a critical priority. However, assessing these effects in industrial workflows remains difficult due to observability barriers. We study the impact of AI-generated code on production quality within a large enterprise operating global products relied upon by billions of users daily. Driven by this scale and user trust, the organization values code quality and has built thorough observability for every line of code deployed into production, enabling us to overcome measurement barriers to assess these effects.
  This study presents a large-scale empirical analysis of AI-generated C++ code from April 2025 to April 2026, tracking 3.52 million code changes across this enterprise's brownfield codebase. The core purpose is to understand the quality, performance, and maintenance characteristics of AI-generated code compared to human-written code in a production environment at scale. We find that AI-generated C++ code has a distinct quality profile, showing higher rates of interface and coupling burdens, copy and allocation overheads, and a reliance on explicit loops over optimized standard APIs. These issues translate into tangible downstream costs, including increased review effort and a 5-8% increase in compute resource consumption. However, we demonstrate that providing models with targeted, taxonomy-informed feedback can mitigate these effects, leading to an 11.1% reduction in targeted static analysis warnings and improved computational efficiency.

</details>


### [185] [Online Monitoring and Corrective Steering of Programming Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06701)
*Shuyang Liu,Saman Dehghan,Ji Young Kim,Jatin Ganhotra,Martin Hirzel,Reyhaneh Jabbarvand*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LivePlan提出了一种实时监控、检测和纠正智能体在GitHub issue修复任务中行为低效和漂移的方法，通过将"评判"与"建议"解耦——确定性规则监控器先检测问题，仅在检测到问题时才调用LLM顾问进行下一步修正，在SWE-bench上最高提升15.2%的解决率，额外成本仅$0.08/实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模项目的GitHub issue修复是长时程任务，需要跨多个位置修改，且issue描述常缺乏定位和修复所需信息。现有智能体在长轨迹执行中容易产生行为低效和漂移：偏离原计划、重复失败动作或在未生成有效补丁前终止。先前方法存在误导性重新规划和昂贵干预的问题。

Method: LivePlan将评判与建议解耦：采用确定性、基于规则的监控器检查轨迹中的通用信号来检测问题（不调用LLM），仅当检测到问题时才咨询顾问LLM获取高层级的下一步修正建议。该方法在SWE-agent基础上实现，使用五种LLM（三种作为执行智能体，两种作为顾问）进行评估。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified和SWE-bench Pro上的评估显示，相比vanilla SWE-agent，LivePlan显著提升issue解决率，一致增益最高达15.2%（平均9.9%），额外成本仅为每实例$0.08。新增的解决方案集中在中等和困难实例上，并一致优于替代方法，对已成功运行的回归最小，且能解决基线无法解决的新问题。

Conclusion: LivePlan通过解耦评判与建议的架构，实现了低开销、高有效性的实时行为纠正，在代码智能体长时程任务中取得显著且稳健的提升。

Abstract: Fixing GitHub issues in large-scale projects is a long-horizon task, especially when a fix requires changes across multiple locations or the issue description lacks the information needed to localize and repair it. As a result, agents traverse long trajectories that are prone to inefficiency and error: they drift away from their intended plan, repeat failed actions, or terminate without a working patch. This paper proposes LivePlan to monitor, detect, and correct such behavioral inefficiencies and drifts in real time. LivePlan decouples judging from advising: a deterministic, rule-based monitor examines general signals over the trajectory to detect issues without invoking an LLM, and only when an issue is detected does it consult an advisor LLM for a high-level, next-step correction. This design avoids the misleading re-planning and costly interventions of prior approaches. We implement LivePlan on top of SWE-agent and evaluate it using five LLMs (three as executor agents and two as advisors) across SWE-bench Verified and SWE-bench Pro. Compared to vanilla SWE-agent, LivePlan notably improves issue resolution rates, achieving consistent gains of up to 15.2% (average: 9.9%), while incurring only an additional cost of $0.08 per instance. The additional solutions concentrate on medium and hard instances. LivePlan consistently outperforms alternative approaches in resolution rate, with minimal regression on already successful runs and new successes on problems that no baseline solves.

</details>


### [186] [Translation Tag Team: Formal Rules and LLMs Translate More Macros Together than Apart](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06705)
*Brent Pappas,Joseph Zalusky,Zachary Burkett,Paul Gazzillo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了MerC，第一个正式规范的宏翻译器，以及MacroBench基准测试集，用于将C语言宏自动翻译为Rust。实验表明MerC支持50%的宏用例，LLM虽能翻译更多但有8%-28%的错误率，而先运行MerC再用LLM处理剩余部分的"接力"策略效果最佳，失败率比纯LLM低32%，翻译量比纯MerC多51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C语言缺乏内存安全性，研究者尝试自动将C翻译为Rust等安全语言。但真实C代码中包含大量宏（macros），现有方法通过预处理将宏内联展开后翻译，导致翻译结果与原始代码结构差异大，丢失了宏的使用形式。因此需要一种能够保留宏结构并直接翻译宏的技术。

Method: （1）研究宏与C共享的语言特性，提取出形式化规范，构建MerC宏翻译器；（2）基于真实C程序中随机采样的宏，构建MacroBench基准测试集；（3）对比MerC与LLM在宏翻译任务上的表现，并提出先运行MerC再用LLM处理剩余宏的"tag team"组合策略。

Result: MerC支持MacroBench中50%的宏测试用例；LLM比MerC多翻译22%-77%的用例，但其中8%-28%的翻译是错误的，需要开发者额外验证；而MerC只产生正确的翻译。组合策略（先MerC后LLM）比纯LLM的失败率平均低32%，同时比纯MerC多翻译平均51%的测试用例。

Conclusion: 宏翻译是C到Rust自动翻译中此前被忽视的重要任务。MerC作为正式规范的工具确保翻译正确性，LLM提供了更广的覆盖但需要人工校验，两者结合的tag team策略在覆盖率和正确性之间取得了更优的平衡。

Abstract: Modern critical software infrastructure is largely written in C. Since C lacks memory safety, researchers are investigating automatic translation of C to safer languages like Rust. But real-world C software consists of more than just C code, often using named code fragments called macros which are not part of the C language proper. State-of-the-art techniques avoid translating macros by preprocessing C code first before translating it. But this approach produces translations that are dissimilar to the original C code, because preprocessing inlines all macro definitions. To preserve macro usage in translated code, we study the language features that macros and C share and distill them into the first formally-specified translator, MerC. To evaluate MerC, we introduce the first macro translation benchmark, MacroBench, with test cases based on macros randomly sampled from real-world C programs. We find that MerC supports 50% of MacroBench's macro test cases. We also use MacroBench to evaluate how effective large language models (LLMs) are at performing the previously-unstudied task of macro translation. LLMs translate 22% to 77% more of MacroBench than MerC, but with 8% and 28% of these translations being incorrect translations requiring additional validation by developers. In contrast, MerC only produces correct translations. Our key insight is that running MerC first then using LLMs on the remainder reaps greater benefits than using either technique alone. This tag team approach has an average failure rate 32% lower than that of LLMs, while also translating an average of 51% more test cases than MerC.

</details>


### [187] [AgentChaos: Chaos Engineering for Agent Systems via Programmatic Fault Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06790)
*Gou Tan,Zhensu Sun,Jieke Shi,Ting Zhang,Zilong He,Qingfu Wu,Shuai Liang,Weifeng Sun,Junda He,Pengfei Chen,Chuanfu Zhang,Lwin Khin Shar,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AgentChaos是一个混沌工程框架，通过LLM API HTTP层进行运行时、非入侵式的故障注入，系统评估Agent系统在故障下的鲁棒性。实验表明所有Agent系统在故障注入下性能显著下降（pass@1下降高达50个百分点），且鲁棒性取决于系统实现而非模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent系统依赖LLM API进行每次响应，但API可能返回服务器错误、截断响应或损坏内容，这些故障会通过下游Agent传播导致任务失败。现有故障注入方法要么离线进行、需要修改源代码，要么无法修改特定响应字段，缺乏系统性的故障分类，因此需要一套运行时、非侵入式的故障注入框架来全面评估Agent系统的鲁棒性。

Method: AgentChaos在所有Agent系统访问LLM的HTTP接口这一共享层注入故障，无需修改源代码。定义了三类故障（crash崩溃、omission遗漏、value值错误），作用于内容和工具调用字段，在运行时拦截并修改LLM API响应，并通过故障触发验证来过滤未触发的任务，避免低估故障影响。在多种Agent系统、基准和骨干LLM上进行了65种故障配置的系统性评估。

Result: 所有Agent系统在故障注入下均出现性能下降，pass@1最高下降50个百分点。系统排名在不同模型间保持一致，表明鲁棒性由系统实现而非模型能力决定。现有故障诊断方法在故障类型上的准确率低于53%，在故障步骤上的准确率低于56%，仍有较大提升空间。

Conclusion: AgentChaos提供了可控、运行时、非入侵式的LLM API故障注入框架，系统揭示了Agent系统在面对API故障时的脆弱性，为Agent系统开发者提供了实用发现，并指出当前故障诊断方法存在显著不足，为该领域提供了改进方向。

Abstract: Agent systems rely on LLM APIs for every response, but these APIs can return server errors, truncated responses, or corrupted content that propagates through downstream agents and causes task failure. Evaluating robustness under these faults is crucial for reliable deployment. Existing fault injection methods are offline, require source code modification, or cannot modify specific response fields. A comprehensive evaluation also requires a systematic fault taxonomy because different fault types affect downstream agents differently. We propose AgentChaos, a chaos engineering framework for controlled, runtime, non-intrusive LLM API fault injection. Since all agent systems access LLMs through the same HTTP interface, we inject faults at this shared layer without modifying source code. We define crash, omission, and value faults on content and tool call fields, intercept and modify LLM API responses at runtime, and verify whether each fault is triggered to filter untriggered tasks and avoid underestimating fault impact. Evaluations across agent systems, benchmarks, and backbone LLMs under 65 fault configurations show that all systems degrade under fault injection, with pass@1 dropping by up to 50 percentage points. The ranking is consistent across models, suggesting that robustness depends on system implementation rather than model capability. Existing fault diagnosis methods achieve below 53% accuracy on fault type and below 56% on fault step, leaving room for improvement. We further reveal practical findings for agent system developers.

</details>


### [188] [Coupling Planning with Episodic Memory in LLM Agents for Software Issue Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06811)
*Jiahao Zhang,Yifan Zhang,Yu Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Resolving a real software issue with a large language model (LLM) agent is a long repair episode, often tens to hundreds of steps spanning exploration, hypothesis, implementation, and verification. Success depends on both the base model's local reasoning and the agent's ability to maintain an evolving plan and remember observations across phases. Existing repository-level agents typically strengthen planning or memory in isolation, leaving long trajectories vulnerable to stale evidence, repeated failed edits, and verification inferred from the agent's own claims instead of execution evidence. We present PMCoder, an issue-resolution agent that couples a hierarchical phase planner with episodic memory. The coupling is bidirectional: the current plan phase conditions memory retrieval, while memory-derived trajectory statistics inform stuck detection and replanning. When available, issue-reproduction verdicts ground verification progress in execution evidence rather than self-reported completion. On SWE-bench Verified, PMCoder resolves an average of $25$ more cases ($+5.0$pp) than a harness-matched baseline, with gains persisting even where the reproduction gate never fires. Further Verified-500 evaluations show the same positive direction across Claude Haiku 4.5, DeepSeek-V4-Flash, and an OpenHands port, with at least $14$ additional resolved cases ($+2.8$pp). Separately, evaluation on TerminalWorld's official sample suggests that the plan-memory substrate transfers beyond issue reports. Ablation and trajectory analyses show where the gains come from: coupling planning and memory outperforms either component alone and reduces repeated failed actions, empty-patch exits, and context-window exhaustion.

</details>


### [189] [How Reasoning Shapes Social Bias in LLM-Generated Code?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06829)
*Weifeng Sun,Jieke Shi,Zhou Yang,Yuchen Chen,Hongyan Li,Meng Yan,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究基于推理的代码生成中的社会偏见问题。研究发现推理过程虽然总体上降低了偏见（从0.64降至0.40），但模型间差异显著，且代码质量未得到一致保持（从0.72降至0.59）。基于此，作者提出ProbeDebias框架，在代码生成前检测并重写带偏见的推理痕迹，实现了87.76%的推理偏见检测F1分数，并将代码偏见平均降低83.73%，同时基本保持代码质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在代码生成中可能通过不公平或差异性的方式对待敏感人口属性而产生社会偏见。以往研究主要关注直接代码生成，而推理型代码生成中的偏见问题尚未被充分探索。因此，作者希望首次系统研究推理型代码生成中的社会偏见现象。

Method: 作者在三个人类中心决策场景中评估了9个标准LLM和大推理模型（LRMs）在敏感偏见任务上的表现。基于研究发现，提出ProbeDebias框架，该框架先检测推理痕迹中的偏见，再在代码生成前对带偏见的推理进行重写，从而在生成阶段从根源上缓解偏见。

Result: 研究发现推理总体上可降低偏见（平均偏见率从0.64降至0.40），但效果因模型而异；代码质量平均从0.72降至0.59，未得到一致保持。ProbeDebias在推理偏见检测上达到87.76%的F1分数，代码偏见平均减少83.73%，质量基本保持。相比最优基线，偏见进一步降低52.70%-54.42%，质量提升9.79%-36.79%。

Conclusion: 推理阶段的偏见强烈预测代码的偏见，仅调整生成配置不足以实现稳健的偏见缓解。推理阶段的偏见分析与干预对实现可信赖的代码生成具有重要价值，ProbeDebias框架验证了在推理阶段进行偏见检测与重写的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for code generation, yet generated programs may exhibit social bias through unfair or differential treatment of sensitive demographic attributes. While prior work mainly studies direct code generation, bias in reasoning-based generation remains underexplored. We conduct the first systematic study of social bias in reasoning-based code generation, evaluating 9 standard LLMs and large reasoning models (LRMs) on realistic bias-sensitive tasks across three human-centered decision scenarios. We find that reasoning generally reduces bias, lowering the average bias rate from 0.64 to 0.40, but the effect varies substantially across models. Meanwhile, code quality is not consistently preserved, with the average quality dropping from 0.72 to 0.59. Biased reasoning strongly predicts biased code, and adjusting generation configurations alone is insufficient for robust mitigation. Based on these findings, we propose ProbeDebias, a reasoning-aware framework that detects and rewrites biased reasoning traces before code generation. ProbeDebias achieves 87.76% F1 for reasoning-bias detection and reduces code bias by 83.73% on average while largely preserving quality. Compared with SOTA baselines, it further reduces average bias by 52.70%-54.42% and improves quality by 9.79%-36.79%. These results highlight the value of reasoning-stage analysis for trustworthy code generation.

</details>


### [190] [Statistical Analysis of Executability and Program Equivalence in Decompilation for IoT Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06960)
*Minami Yoda,Jialong Li,Yasuyuki Tahara,Yuichi Sei,Yutaka Matsuno*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种九维质量评估指标（涵盖结构、行为和语义三类相似度），用于量化LLM驱动的IoT固件反编译代码的质量。基于OpenWrt平台318个程序、5种反编译方法生成的19,625个反编译结果，统计分析表明可重编译成功组的整体得分显著高于失败组（Cohen's d=0.92）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT设备处理敏感隐私信息（音频、视频、认证数据），固件漏洞检测至关重要。LLM反编译虽然在可读性和可重编译率上表现优异，但其概率性token预测倾向于优先保证语法正确性，可能生成看似合理但语义与原始二进制不同的代码。现有评估指标主要关注测试用例通过情况，无法充分识别内部结构被改变但行为表现看似正确的代码，因此需要从多角度量化反编译代码内部结构的评估指标。

Method: 提出九维质量评估指标，分为三类：结构性（structural）、行为性（behavioral）和语义性（semantic）相似度。选取OpenWrt（开源路由器平台，众多商用路由器的底层系统）中318个程序，使用五种方法（一种基于规则、四种基于LLM）生成19,625个反编译结果，并进行统计分析。

Result: 可重编译成功组在整体得分上显著高于失败组（Cohen's d=0.92）；其中行为相似度的效应量d=0.96，结构相似度d=0.69，证明这些指标是反编译质量的重要预测因子。

Conclusion: 本研究为量化IoT设备实现缺陷提供了统计评估基础，并提供了一个可泛化到黑盒生成模型质量评估的框架。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) devices handle sensitive privacy-related information such as user audio, video, and authentication data, making it essential to detect vulnerabilities in their firmware. Decompilation, a key detection technique, has recently attracted attention because Large Language Models (LLMs) enable high readability and high recompilation success rates. However, because LLM outputs depend on probabilistic token prediction, they tend to prioritize syntactic correctness and may generate plausible-looking code that is semantically different from the original binary. Vulnerabilities often arise in details that are easily lost in this process, such as error-handling flows and boundary checks. Existing evaluation metrics focus mainly on passing test cases and cannot sufficiently identify code whose internal structure has been altered despite appearing behaviorally valid, so a metric that quantifies the internal structure of decompiled code from multiple perspectives is needed. We propose a nine-dimensional quality evaluation metric consisting of three categories: structural, behavioral, and semantic similarity. Targeting 318 programs from OpenWrt, an open-source router platform underlying many commercial routers, we generated 19,625 decompilation results using five methods (one rule-based and four LLM-based) and analyzed them statistically. The recompilation-success group achieved significantly higher overall scores than the failure group (Cohen's d=0.92); behavioral similarity showed d=0.96 and structural similarity d=0.69, demonstrating that these metrics are important predictors of decompilation quality. This study provides a statistical evaluation foundation for quantifying implementation defects in IoT devices and a framework that generalizes to quality evaluation of black-box generative models.

</details>


### [191] [What Are Developers Actually Discussing When Visual Regression Tests Fail?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07020)
*Miku Watanabe,Kosei Horikawa,Brittany Reid,Yutaro Kashiwa,Hajimu Iida*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对307个使用Chromatic进行视觉回归测试（VRT）的PR和299个仅有图片附件无VRT的PR进行实证分析，发现VRT-PRs在验收率上无显著差异，但解决时间更长（3.8倍）、讨论评论更多（10倍）、代码变更更大（1.75-4.5倍）。通过189个VRT标记问题的卡片分类，识别出7类缺陷，其中约18.5%的问题是非风格性的，包括组件状态未定义、内容消失和不可察觉的视觉回归。研究表明VRT不仅是风格检查器，还能作为代码变更意外后果的次级检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉回归测试被广泛用于检测UI中的意外视觉变化，但其实际效果和局限性缺乏系统性实证研究。作者希望通过大规模分析来回答：VRT是否真的只捕获风格回归？它相对于仅有图片（无VRT）的PR在审查流程中表现如何？VRT能否捕获非风格性的缺陷？

Method: 从103个GitHub仓库中收集307个包含Chromatic VRT结果的PR和299个仅含图片附件无VRT的PR，进行定量对比（验收率、解决时间、评论数、代码变更规模）。对189个VRT标记的问题进行卡片分类（card-sorting）分析，识别出七类缺陷。同时记录VRT检测到来自无关文件代码变更所导致视觉回归的案例。

Result: 定量上，VRT-PRs与Visual PRs在验收率上无显著差异，但VRT-PRs中位解决时间长3.8倍、评论多10倍、代码变更大1.75-4.5倍。VRT结果通常在审查过程中期分享，持续推动讨论而非仅作为最终检查。七类缺陷分布为：布局39.7%、外观27.5%、颜色14.8%、文本9.5%、状态6.9%、测试6.3%、图像4.2%。约18.5%的问题涉及非风格性缺陷，包括未定义组件状态（13例）、内容消失（17例）、不可察觉的视觉回归（5例）。

Conclusion: 视觉回归测试除主要作为风格检查器外，还充当代码变更意外后果的次级检测器，能够捕捉到非局部的、任何定向测试都可能遗漏的视觉回归。这为VRT在维护工具链中的集成方式提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Visual Regression Tests (VRTs) are widely adopted as a mechanism for detecting unintended visual changes in user interfaces. By design, VRTs operate on rendered pixel output, and the prevailing assumption is that they catch stylistic regressions such as layout shifts, color mismatches, and font alterations. We conduct an empirical analysis of 307 pull requests (PRs) from 103 GitHub repositories that incorporate VRT results via Chromatic, comparing them against 299 PRs that contain image attachments but no VRT (Visual PRs). Quantitatively, VRT-PRs show no significant acceptance-rate difference, but exhibit a 3.8 times longer median resolution time, 10 times more discussion comments, and 1.75 to 4.5 times larger code changes than Visual PRs. VRT results are typically shared around the midpoint of the review process, sustaining ongoing discussion rather than serving only as a final check. Through a card-sorting analysis of 189 VRT-flagged issues, we identify seven defect categories assigned to the analyzed issues: Layout (39.7\%), Appearance (27.5\%), Color (14.8\%), Text (9.5\%), State (6.9\%), Test (6.3\%), and Image (4.2\%). The three most frequent categories are stylistic, while approximately 18.5\% of analyzed issues (35/189) involve non-stylistic origins, including undefined component state (13 cases), content disappearance (17 cases across multiple categories), and visually imperceptible regressions (5 cases). We further document cases in which VRT detected visual regressions originating from code changes in seemingly unrelated files, exposing non-local effects that no targeted test would have been written to catch. These observations indicate that, in addition to its primary role as a stylistic checker, VRT functions as a secondary detector of unintended consequences of code changes, with implications for how VRT should be integrated into the maintenance toolchain.

</details>


### [192] [CAS2UML: A Handwritten Sketch-to-PlantUML Dataset for Class and Activity Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07036)
*Simon Scholz,Mersedeh Sadeghi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CAS2UML数据集，包含557个手绘UML图（271个类图和286个活动图），每个图均配有人工验证的PlantUML代码，并提供基于PlantUML的验证工具和可复用脚本，用于检查生成UML工件的语法正确性和可渲染性，从而支持草图到UML方法的重现性基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型和多模态AI的兴起，从草图和图像自动生成UML受到越来越多的关注。然而，由于缺乏带有可执行groundtruth模型的公开数据集，可重现的评估仍然困难。作者旨在解决这一评估瓶颈。

Method: 构建CAS2UML数据集，收集557个手绘UML图（271个类图+286个活动图），为每张图人工验证生成对应的PlantUML代码；同时提供基于PlantUML的验证工具和可复用脚本，用于检查语法正确性和可渲染性，实现草图到UML方法的可重现基准测试。

Result: 公开了数据集（Hugging Face）、验证工具与处理脚本（GitHub）以及演示视频（YouTube），支持社区对草图到UML方法进行可重现的评估与基准测试。

Conclusion: CAS2UML填补了草图到UML评估领域公开数据集的空白，通过提供带可执行groundtruth的数据和验证工具链，使得该领域的研究评估更具可重现性和可比性。

Abstract: Automated UML generation from sketches and images is gaining renewed attention with the rise of large language models and multimodal AI. However, reproducible evaluation remains difficult due to the lack of public datasets with executable groundtruth models. We present CAS2UML, a public dataset of 557 handdrawn UML diagrams, including 271 class diagrams and 286 activity diagrams, each paired with manually validated PlantUML code. We also provide a PlantUML-based validation tool and reusable scripts for checking the syntactic correctness and renderability of generated UML artifacts, enabling reproducible benchmarking of sketch-to-UML approaches. The dataset, validation tool, processing scripts, documentation, and demonstration video are publicly available at: Dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Seym0n /cas2uml_hand-drawn_to_plantuml_dataset; Tool and Scripts: https://github.com/Seym0n/handwritten-uml-dataset; Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=KQrYeGgT3hs.

</details>


### [193] [Beyond Text Matching: Towards Reference-Free Evaluation for Human-Oriented Binary Reverse Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07038)
*Xiuwei Shang,Li Hu,Xiao Jiang,Jieke Shi,Junda He,Zhou Yang,Shaoyin Cheng,Guoqiang Chen,Weiming Zhang,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了LLM-as-a-Judge范式在人类导向的二进制逆向工程（HOBRE）评估中的应用，提出了专家标注的免参考基准BinJudgeBench，并设计了轻量级路由机制BinJudge来为每个任务和样本自适应选择最优评估配置，将评估与人类专家的相关性提升4.5%-24.7%，同时将API成本降至静态最优配置的0.06-0.84倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HOBRE旨在将反编译伪代码转化为更易人类理解的形式，但其输出评估一直面临根本性挑战：人工评估成本高、难以扩展；现有自动化指标要么需要可执行测试用例和运行时环境（真实二进制中通常不可用），要么依赖高质量的源代码参考（通常不可访问且无法捕捉语义等价但词汇多样的输出）。虽然LLM-as-a-Judge范式天然适合HOBRE评估，但其有效性尚未被系统探索。

Method: （1）构建BinJudgeBench：首个专家标注的、免参考的多维度人工判断评估基准，覆盖三个代表性任务：函数名恢复、二进制代码摘要和解编译优化。（2）系统分析评估器的配置因素（backbone LLM、提示策略、解码温度），发现不存在适用于所有情况的"一刀切"配置。（3）提出BinJudge：采用轻量级路由机制，为每个任务和样本自适应选择最优评估配置。

Result: LLM-as-a-Judge在BinJudgeBench上与人类判断的平均相关性达63.20%，远超传统自动化指标的35.04%。BinJudge进一步将与人类专家的相关性提升4.5%-24.7%，并将API成本降至静态最优配置的0.06-0.84倍，在三个任务上均验证了其可扩展性和成本效益。

Conclusion: LLM-as-a-Judge范式是HOBRE评估的高保真且可扩展的替代方案，但不存在通用的最优配置；通过BinJudge的路由机制可以实现任务和样本级别的自适应配置选择，在保持高评估保真度的同时显著降低计算成本，为HOBRE提供了可扩展、经济高效且高保真的自动化评估方案。

Abstract: Human-Oriented Binary Reverse Engineering (HOBRE) aims to transform decompiled pseudocode into a more human-friendly representation, thereby reducing the cognitive burden of reverse analysis and improving efficiency. However, reliably evaluating HOBRE outputs remains a fundamental challenge: human evaluation is costly, time-consuming, and difficult to scale, while existing automated metrics either require executable test cases and runtime environments that are often unavailable for real-world binaries, or rely on high-quality source code references that are typically inaccessible and fail to capture semantically equivalent but lexically diverse outputs. Although LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm is naturally well-suited to HOBRE evaluation, its effectiveness remains underexplored.
  This paper presents the first systematic investigation of the LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm for HOBRE across three representative tasks: function name recovery, binary code summarization, and decompilation optimization. We introduce BinJudgeBench, the first expert-annotated, reference-free evaluation benchmark based on multi-dimensional human judgment, where LLM-as-a-Judge achieves an average correlation of 63.20\% with human judgment, outperforming traditional automated metrics at 35.04\%. By analyzing judge configurations across backbone LLMs, prompting strategies, and decoding temperatures, we find that no ``one-size-fits-all'' configuration exists, as the optimal setup varies across tasks and individual samples. To address this, we propose BinJudge, which employs a lightweight routing mechanism to adaptively select the optimal judge configuration for each task and sample. BinJudge improves correlation with human experts by 4.5\%-24.7\% and reduces API cost to 0.06$\times$-0.84$\times$ of that of static best configurations, providing a scalable, cost-effective, and high-fidelity automated evaluation scheme for HOBRE.

</details>


### [194] [Explanation-Guided Metamorphic Testing of Specialized Language Models: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07076)
*Xingcheng Chen,Mehmet Besenk,Andrea Stocco*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究，验证了可解释性引导的蜕变测试在检测任务专用语言模型鲁棒性方面优于启发式变异策略，生成验证过的失败诱发测试用例数量高出2.30倍，并揭示了模型对命名实体和格式线索的系统性捷径行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务专用语言模型已广泛应用于软件工程垂直领域，但缺乏关于如何在语义保持输入变换下测试其鲁棒性和检测脆弱行为的实证证据。

Method: 在3个数据集、4种模型架构和20种测试配置上开展大规模实证研究，结合归因方法、token优先级排序、LLM驱动变异和自动化语义验证生成语言有效的测试变体，与启发式基线进行对比评估。

Result: 可解释性引导的蜕变测试比启发式变异策略多生成2.30倍的已验证失败诱发测试用例；语义验证显著提升变异有效性，并通过人工标注在门控接受的变体中达到高标签保持精度；同时揭示了模型对命名实体和格式线索的过度依赖等系统性捷径行为。

Conclusion: 可解释性引导的蜕变测试是评估垂直AI应用中任务专用语言模型鲁棒性的有效且实用的方法。

Abstract: \head{Background} Task-specialized language models are increasingly integrated into software engineering workflows to support vertical-domain activities such as issue triaging, document classification, and automated analysis. Despite their adoption, there is limited empirical evidence on how to test their robustness and detect brittle behaviors under semantics-preserving input transformations.
  \head{Aims} This paper investigates whether explainability-guided metamorphic testing can improve the effectiveness and validity of robustness testing for specialized language models compared to heuristic mutation strategies.
  \head{Method} We conduct a large-scale empirical study of explanation-guided metamorphic testing across three datasets, four model architectures, and 20 testing configurations derived from combinations of attribution methods and mutation strategies. The evaluated configurations combine attribution-based token prioritization, LLM-driven mutation, and automated semantic verification to generate linguistically valid test variants. We assess failure discovery capability, semantic validity, and testing efficiency against heuristic baselines.
  \head{Results} Explanation-guided metamorphic testing generates 2.30$\times$ more verified failure-inducing test cases than heuristic mutation strategies. Semantic verification substantially improves mutation validity and achieves high label-preservation precision among gate-accepted variants according to human annotation. The study further reveals systematic shortcut behaviors across models, including over-reliance on named entities and formatting cues.
  \head{Conclusions} The results provide evidence that explanation-guided metamorphic testing is an effective and practical approach for empirically evaluating the robustness of task-specialized language models used in vertical AI applications.

</details>


### [195] [Rust Coreutils: Rebuilding Unix Foundations in a Modern Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07135)
*Sylvestre Ledru,Samuel Tardieu,Stefano Zacchiroli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文回顾了Rust coreutils的开发历程——一个用Rust语言对GNU coreutils（包含约100个基础命令如ls、cp、cat的UNIX核心工具包）进行的现代重实现。该项目已达到可替代GNU coreutils、兼容大多数Linux发行版的水平。通过对比两者，论文探讨了如何有效重实现关键软件，以及现代编程特性如何吸引开发者对遗留软件产生兴趣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNU coreutils是UNIX系统中的关键软件包，承载着每天在数百万台计算机上运行的约100个基础命令，但其C代码库源于20世纪90年代初，属于功能完备的传统遗留软件。作者希望探索如何有效重实现这一关键包，并研究现代编程语言特性能否为遗留软件注入新的开发活力。

Method: 论文通过实际开发Rust coreutils这一开源项目，用Rust语言重新实现GNU coreutils的各个命令，并将其与原始GNU coreutils进行系统性对比分析，从而提炼出创建关键软件可靠替代品的实践经验和方法论。

Result: Rust coreutils成功达到了GNU coreutils的drop-in replacement（即插即用替代品）状态，可与大多数Linux发行版兼容，证明了用现代编程语言重实现关键遗留软件是可行且有效的。

Conclusion: 重实现关键软件需要系统性对比与严谨的方法论，而现代编程语言特性（如Rust的内存安全、类型系统等）不仅能提升软件质量，还能显著吸引开发者社区对遗留软件包的关注和贡献热情。

Abstract: GNU core utilities (coreutils) is a crucial package in modern UNIX systems. It comprises around 100 fundamental commands---like ls, cp, and cat---which run every day on millions of computers. However, GNU coreutils is also legacy software, with its C codebase dating back to the early 1990s and arguably feature-complete. If one were to consider reimplementing this essential package, how would they do so effectively, and why? This paper recounts the development of Rust coreutils, a contemporary open source reimplementation of GNU coreutils in the Rust programming language, which has reached the status of a drop-in replacement for GNU coreutils, compatible with most Linux distributions. By comparing Rust coreutils with its ancestor, we offer insights into creating a reliable substitute for critical software and highlight how modern programming features can attract development interest in legacy packages.

</details>


### [196] [Towards Assurance Closure in AI-Native Large-Scale Agile Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07317)
*Ricardo Britto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"AI原生宣言"愿景下的敏捷软件开发需要"保证闭环"（assurance closure）机制，即在AI执行更多工程流程时，系统需能建立必要条件、获取证据、判断证据可信度、维护其有效性并据此约束代理权限。文章识别了六个残余缺口，提出一个基于共享语义保证层的六能力高层架构，并给出四个研究问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI原生软件开发（AI-native software development）的最终状态不能仅靠更好的代码生成达成。随着人类从执行者转向治理意图、风险与例外，系统必须能够自主建立并维护"保证"（assurance），否则无法安全地在人机协同（human-on-the-loop）模式下大规模部署智能代理。

Method: 文章系统梳理了现有保证机制（形式化方法、测试、仿真、保证案例、数字孪生、运行时保证），从中识别出六个使保证推理难以机器可操作的残余缺口，据此提出一个高层架构，架构包含六个对应能力，构建在一个共享的语义保证层之上，并归纳出四个研究问题以指导后续落地。

Result: 识别出六个残余差距并提出了六能力架构：通过共享语义保证层整合保证推理，使保证闭环对机器可操作；提出了四个研究问题以将该架构转化为可信赖的、人在回路中的AI原生研发体系。

Conclusion: 实现AI原生敏捷开发需要从单纯提升代码生成转向建立机器可操作的保证推理体系。通过弥合六项缺口、部署六项能力并回答四个研究问题，可以构建起依赖可靠的、人类监督下的AI原生研发环境。

Abstract: The AI-Native Manifesto envisions large-scale agile software development in which humans increasingly govern intent, risk, and exceptions while agents execute more of the engineering process. Realizing that end-state requires more than better code generation: it requires assurance closure, meaning that the system can establish what must be true, determine and obtain appropriate evidence, judge the credibility of that evidence, preserve its validity through change, and use the resulting uncertainty to bound agent authority. Existing work already provides many of the necessary mechanisms across formal methods, testing, simulation, assurance cases, digital twins, and runtime assurance. We identify six residual gaps in making the surrounding assurance reasoning sufficiently machine-operable, propose a high-level architecture with six corresponding capabilities built on a shared semantic assurance layer, and formulate four research questions to turn that architecture into dependable, human-on-the-loop, AI-native R&D.

</details>


### [197] [PACE: Primitive-Aware Code Evolution for Automated Algorithm Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07395)
*Zhuoliang Xie,Ruihao Zheng,Xiang Xu,Genghui Li,Zhengkun Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE（Primitive-Aware Code Evolution），一种基于大语言模型的自动化算法设计方法，通过将算法分解为可执行的算法原语（EAPs）来实现局部逻辑与完整程序的解耦，使得有价值的代码片段能够在算法进化过程中被保留和跨程序迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的自动化算法设计通常将算法视为完整的、不可分割的程序进行进化。这种"整体程序"视角虽然简化了搜索空间，但将有用的局部逻辑与宿主程序紧密耦合，导致有价值的代码片段在整体程序被丢弃时随之消失，难以评估单个算法组件的贡献。

Method: PACE通过三个关键机制实现：1）将局部逻辑表示为持久化的可执行算法原语（EAPs），实现与完整程序的解耦；2）维护一个动态的EAP集合，支持代码级别的跨程序迁移；3）采用原始子感知的进化算子，从结构上保证这些组件的保留和跨程序传递。此外，利用基于父代相对性能提升的Thompson采样来引导原语选择，无需额外评估数据集。

Result: 在四个任务上的实验表明，PACE能够有效发现具有竞争力的算法，同时在结构上保留有价值的算法组件。

Conclusion: PACE通过原语级别的算法进化，解决了传统整体程序进化中局部逻辑难以评估和复用的核心问题，为LLM驱动的自动化算法设计提供了更细粒度的进化范式。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based automated algorithm design typically evolves algorithms as complete, indivisible programs. While this whole-program perspective simplifies the search space, it fundamentally couples the useful local logic to its host program. Consequently, valuable code snippets vanish when the overall program is discarded, making it highly difficult to assess the contribution of individual algorithmic components.To address this, we propose Primitive-Aware Code Evolution (PACE), which decouples local logic from complete programs by representing it as persistent units called Executable Algorithmic Primitives (EAPs). To enable code-level transfer, PACE maintains a dynamic set of EAPs. Algorithm evolution is driven by primitive-aware operators that structurally guarantee the retention and cross-program transfer of these components. To evaluate them effectively, PACE leverages Thompson sampling based on parent-relative performance improvements, guiding primitive selection from the set without requiring extra evaluation datasets. Experiments on four tasks demonstrate that PACE effectively discovers competitive algorithms while structurally preserving valuable algorithmic components.

</details>


### [198] [Circuit-Based Program Verification: Sequential Circuits as an Intermediate Representation for Verifying C Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07397)
*Po-Chun Chien,Nian-Ze Lee,Armin Biere,Dirk Beyer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CPV框架，将C程序翻译为顺序电路，利用现成的硬件模型检查器作为后端进行软件验证。在超过16000个任务的基准测试中，CPV与五个成熟的软件验证器相比表现出有竞争力的性能，并能独特地解决其他验证器无法处理的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件验证和硬件验证虽然都在推理状态转换系统，但两个社区各自发展了独立的中间表示和验证算法，缺乏交叉融合。作者希望利用硬件模型检查技术（如IC3/PDR、k-归纳）来增强软件验证的能力。

Method: CPV是一个模块化框架，将C程序翻译为顺序电路（sequential circuits），将程序的控制流和数据流折叠为单一的整体转换关系，然后调用现成的硬件模型检查器作为后端。支持可达性安全性和终止性分析，集成多种硬件模型检查器（涵盖有界模型检查、k-归纳、IC3/PDR等算法），并将硬件模型检查器发现的反例自动翻译回软件验证证物供用户理解。

Result: 在超过16000个任务的基准测试套件上，CPV与五个成熟的软件验证器相比取得了有竞争力的性能，并展现出互补优势，能够独特地解决其他验证器无法处理的任务。

Conclusion: 顺序电路可以作为软件验证的有效中间表示，硬件模型检查技术能够成功迁移到软件验证领域，两种验证方法之间存在互补性，值得进一步融合与发展。

Abstract: Formal verification of software programs and hardware designs shares the common goal of reasoning about state-transition systems, yet the two communities have largely developed separate intermediate representations and verification algorithms. This paper investigates sequential circuits as an intermediate representation for software verification, with the goal of enabling direct application of hardware-model-checking techniques. We present Circuit-Based Program Verification (CPV), a modular framework that translates C programs into sequential circuits and employs off-the-shelf hardware model checkers as backends. Unlike traditional software verifiers, which typically rely on path-based exploration, CPV reasons over sequential circuits, where a program's control and data flows are folded into a monolithic transition relation that can be analyzed as a whole. The framework supports reachability-safety and termination analyses and integrates multiple state-of-the-art hardware model checkers, which together provide access to diverse verification algorithms, including bounded model checking, $k$-induction, and IC3/PDR. Counterexamples found by hardware model checkers are automatically translated back into software-verification witnesses for users to interpret verification results. We conducted a comprehensive evaluation on a benchmark suite of more than 16000 tasks. Our results show that CPV achieved competitive performance against five well-established software verifiers and exhibited complementary strengths by uniquely solving tasks that other verifiers cannot handle.

</details>


### [199] [Taxonomy-Driven Analysis of Open-Source AI Risk Mitigation Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07446)
*Afreen Alam,Evgenija Popchanovska,Ana Gjorgjevikj,Maryan Rizinski,Lubomir T. Chitkushev,Irena Vodenska,Dimitar Trajanov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分类学的结构化协议来自动化AI风险缓解工作，将21个主流开源LLM评估与安全工具的能力映射到扩展MIT AI风险缓解与响应分类法的32个子类别上，通过LLM辅助的RAG流水线分析工具源代码和文档，揭示了工具能力分布严重不均衡的现状，并提出了分层风险缓解架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在企业场景中的快速应用，运营、安全和治理风险不断增加。现有工具虽然支持模型评估、对抗性测试、运行时护栏和可观测性，但工具生态碎片化严重，工具通常针对特定工程任务设计，其技术术语与治理框架和风险分类法不对齐，导致难以判断哪些工具解决哪些风险、关键缺口在哪里，手动识别和缓解风险难以规模化。

Method: 提出了一套基于分类学的结构化AI风险缓解协议，包含：(1) 将21个主流开源LLM评估与安全工具的能力映射到扩展MIT AI风险缓解与响应分类法的32个子类别；(2) 构建LLM辅助的检索增强生成（RAG）流水线来分析工具源代码和文档，提取各分类类别的能力；(3) 通过三名独立评审员进行可靠性评估（Fleiss' Kappa = 0.509）；(4) 采用多数投票机制进行映射。

Result: 映射协议的F1分数达到75.5%（多数投票后）。可靠性评估显示三名独立评审员之间达到中等一致性。分析揭示了高度倾斜的工具能力分布：工具主要集中在技术和运营控制领域，而治理、法律与监管、金融与市场控制等类别在现有开源工具中基本未被覆盖。

Conclusion: 研究提供了企业AI风险类别与开源缓解能力之间的实用映射，识别出需要人工监督的领域，提出了结合基于工具的控制与组织和监管流程的分层风险缓解架构，并提供了一个适用于开源和专有解决方案的分类学驱动框架。

Abstract: Rapid adoption of large language models (LLMs) in enterprise settings has introduced operational, security, and governance risks. As generative AI applications move from pilot to production, manual harm identification and mitigation are becoming difficult to scale. Although many tools support model evaluation, adversarial testing, runtime guardrails, and observability, the tooling landscape remains fragmented. Tools are typically designed for specific engineering tasks and described in technical terms that do not align with governance frameworks or risk taxonomies, making it difficult to determine which tools address which risks and where critical gaps remain. This paper proposes a structured protocol to automate AI risk mitigation through a taxonomy-driven analysis of open-source LLM evaluation and security tools. We map the capabilities of 21 prominent open-source tools to the 32 subcategories of the extended MIT AI Risk Mitigation and Response Taxonomy. An LLM-assisted retrieval-augmented generation pipeline analyzes source code and documentation to extract capabilities for each taxonomy category. Reliability assessment yielded moderate agreement (Fleiss' Kappa = 0.509) among three independent reviewers. The analysis reveals a highly skewed landscape in which tools cluster around technical and operational controls, while governance, legal and regulatory, and financial and market controls remain largely unaddressed. This motivates a layered risk-mitigation architecture combining tool-based controls with organizational and regulatory processes. The mapping protocol achieved an F1 score of 75.5% after majority voting. Overall, the study provides a practical mapping between enterprise AI risk categories and open-source mitigation capabilities, identifies where human oversight remains necessary, and presents a taxonomy-driven framework applicable to open-source and proprietary solutions.

</details>
